# Phase25 — Source clause traceability

This map is navigation plus reviewed adoption disposition, not runtime proof. All target section IDs are in the normative contracts; all story IDs are in the acceptance register. Source IDs retain question/evidence namespaces, avoiding old A1/A01 observation collisions. Unnumbered addenda have explicit trace aliases. Historic stage instructions are superseded only by the current explicit spec-publication authorization.

The [source map](source-map.json) records original and adopted hashes. Source line numbers are historical coordinates; links navigate to the adopted source heading. The [decision log](decision-log.md) records ratification and the [final assembly review](research/phase25-implementation-clarifications.md) resolves cross-question amendments.
